00878: function buildDrawRef({ draw, lotteryKey }) {
00879:   const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
00880: 
00881:   const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(
00882:     draw?.close_hour || "",
00883:     lotteryKey
00884:   );
00885: 
00886:   const lotteryName = String(draw?.lottery_name || draw?.name || "SEM_NOME").trim();
00887: 
00888:   const lotteryIdFromDraw = pickLotteryId(draw);
00889:   const lotteryIdPart = safeIdPart(lotteryIdFromDraw || lotteryName);
00890: 
00891:   // ✅ ID por SLOT
00892:   const drawId = `${safeIdPart(lotteryKey)}__${date}__${safeIdPart(
00893:     closeSlot
00894:   )}__${lotteryIdPart}`;
00895:   const drawRef = db.collection("draws").doc(drawId);
00896: 
00897:   return {
00898:     drawId,
00899:     drawRef,
00900:     date,

00902:     closeSlot,
00903:     lotteryName,
00904:     lotteryIdFromDraw,
00905:   };
00906: }

00908: /**
00909:  * Prova forte de “já completo”:
00910:  * - draw existe
00911:  * - prizesCount > 0 (ou) existe ao menos 1 doc na subcoleção prizes
00912:  */
00913: async function checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef) {
00914:   try {
00915:     const dSnap = await drawRef.get();
00916:     if (!dSnap.exists) return false;
00917: 
00918:     const data = dSnap.data() || {};
00919:     if (Number(data.prizesCount) > 0) return true;
00920: 
00921:     // fallback: olha 1 doc de prizes
00922:     const pSnap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").limit(1).get();
00923:     return !pSnap.empty;
00924:   } catch {
00925:     return false;
00926:   }
00927: }
00928: 
00929: /**
00930:  * ✅ Prova forte por SLOT (anti-índice composto)
00931:  * - Query filtra SOMENTE por date
00932:  * - close_hour + lottery_key/lotteryKey filtrados em memória
00933:  *
00934:  * ✅ FIX CRÍTICO:
00935:  * - Normaliza o close_hour armazenado (pode existir legado "09:09")
00936:  *   e compara por SLOT (HH:00), para não “perder” doc existente.
00937:  */
00938: async function checkSlotCompletion({ date, closeHour, lotteryKey }) {
00939:   try {
00940:     const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00941:     const targetSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
00942:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00943: 
00944:     // query mínima
00945:     const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
00946:     if (snap.empty) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00947: 
00948:     const refs = [];
00949:     snap.forEach((doc) => {
00950:       const d = doc.data() || {};
00951: 
00952:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00953:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00954: 
00955:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;
00956: 
00957:       // ✅ compara por SLOT normalizado (pega legado "09:09" como "09:00")
00958:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;
00959:       if (storedSlot !== targetSlot) return;
00960: 
00961:       refs.push(doc.ref);
00962:     });
00963: 
00964:     if (!refs.length) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00965: 
00966:     let complete = 0;
00967:     for (const ref of refs) {
00968:       const ok = await checkAlreadyComplete(ref);
00969:       if (ok) complete += 1;
00970:     }
00971: 
00972:     return { docs: refs.length, complete };
00973:   } catch {
00974:     return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00975:   }
00976: }
00977: 
00978: /**
00979:  * Importa payload para o Firestore.
00980:  * closeHour (opcional): se informado, importa SOMENTE draws daquele horário (SLOT).
00981:  *
00982:  * Opts extras:
00983:  * - skipIfAlreadyComplete (boolean): se true, e o draw do closeHour já estiver completo no FS,
00984:  *   então NÃO escreve nada (evita ruído e falsos positivos).
00985:  */
00986: async function importFromPayload({
00987:   payload,
00988:   lotteryKey,
00989:   closeHour = null,
00990:   skipIfAlreadyComplete = false,
00991: } = {}) {
00992:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00993:   const uf = resolveUfFromLotteryKey(lk); // ✅ UF correta (ex.: RJ)
00994: 
00995:   let batch = db.batch();
00996:   let ops = 0;
00997: 
00998:   let totalDrawsFromApi = 0;
00999:   let totalDrawsMatchedClose = 0;
01000:   let totalDrawsValid = 0;
01001: 
01002:   let totalDrawsUpserted = 0;
01003:   let totalDrawsSaved = 0;
01004: 
01005:   let totalPrizesUpserted = 0;
01006:   let totalPrizesSaved = 0;
01007: 
01008:   let skippedInvalid = 0;
01009:   let skippedEmpty = 0;
01010:   let skippedCloseHour = 0;
01011:   let skippedAlreadyComplete = 0;
01012: 
01013:   // Provas úteis para o scheduler (quando closeHour é informado)
01014:   const proof = {
01015:     filterClose: null,
01016: 
01017:     apiHasPrizes: false,
01018:     apiReturnedTargetDraws: 0, // draws válidos do closeHour com prizes
01019:     targetDrawIds: [],
01020:     inferredDate: null, // date do slot (inferido a partir do payload)
01021: 
01022:     expectedTargets: 0, // = max(1, apiReturnedTargetDraws)
01023:     slotDocsFound: 0,
01024:     alreadyCompleteCount: 0,
01025:     alreadyCompleteAny: false,
01026:     alreadyCompleteAll: false,
01027: 
01028:     targetWriteCount: 0,
01029:     targetSavedCount: 0,
01030:   };
01031: 
01032:   // ✅ filtro deve ser SLOT também
01033:   const filterClose = closeHour
01034:     ? normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour, lk).slot
01035:     : null;
01036:   if (filterClose && !isHHMM(filterClose)) {
01037:     throw new Error('Parâmetro "closeHour" inválido. Use HH:MM.');
01038:   }
01039:   proof.filterClose = filterClose;
01040: 
01041:   const apiArr = Array.isArray(payload?.data) ? payload.data : [];
01042: 
01043:   for (const draw of apiArr) {
01044:     totalDrawsFromApi++;
01045: 
01046:     const date = String(draw?.date || "").trim();
01047:     const { raw: closeRaw, slot: closeSlot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(
01048:       draw?.close_hour || "",
01049:       lk
01050:     );
01051: 
01052:     // filtro por horário (SLOT)
01053:     if (filterClose) {
01054:       if (closeSlot !== filterClose) {
01055:         skippedCloseHour++;
01056:         continue;
01057:       }
01058:     }
01059: 
01060:     totalDrawsMatchedClose++;
01061: 
01062:     // validações mínimas (slot precisa ser HH:MM)
01063:     if (!isISODate(date) || !isHHMM(closeSlot)) {
01064:       skippedInvalid++;
01065:       continue;
01066:     }
01067: 
01068:     // prizes existentes
01069:     const prizes = extractPrizes(draw);
01070: 
01071:     // Se não tem prize nenhum, NÃO grava
01072:     if (!prizes.length) {
01073:       skippedEmpty++;
01074:       continue;
01075:     }
01076: 
01077:     totalDrawsValid++;
01078: 
01079:     if (filterClose) {
01080:       proof.apiHasPrizes = true;
01081:       proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws += 1;
01082:       proof.inferredDate = proof.inferredDate || date;
01083:     }
01084: 
01085:     const { drawId, drawRef, lotteryName, lotteryIdFromDraw } = buildDrawRef({
01086:       draw,
01087:       lotteryKey: lk,
01088:     });
01089: 
01090:     if (filterClose) proof.targetDrawIds.push(drawId);
01091: 
01092:     // Em closeHour: checa “já completo” e, se configurado, pula escrita (por draw)
01093:     if (filterClose) {
01094:       const already = await checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef);
01095:       if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && already) {
01096:         skippedAlreadyComplete++;
01097:         continue;
01098:       }
01099:     }
01100: 
01101:     // métrica "novo" (opcional — custa reads)
01102:     let isNewDraw = false;
01103:     if (CHECK_EXISTENCE) {
01104:       try {
01105:         const snap = await drawRef.get();
01106:         isNewDraw = !snap.exists;
01107:       } catch {
01108:         isNewDraw = false;
01109:       }
01110:     }
01111: 
01112:     const ymd = date;
01113: 
01114:     batch.set(
01115:       drawRef,
01116:       {
01117:         source: "kingapostas",
01118: 
01119:         uf: uf || null,
01120:         lottery_key: lk,
01121: 
01122:         lottery_name: lotteryName,
01123:         lottery_id: lotteryIdFromDraw || null,
01124:         drawId,
01125: 
01126:         date,
01127:         ymd,
01128: 
01129:         // ✅ campo de negócio (SLOT)
01130:         close_hour: closeSlot,
01131: 
01132:         // ✅ compat: campos usados no front (evita undefined)
01133:         hour: closeSlot ? String(closeSlot).slice(0, 2) : null,
01134:         close: closeSlot || null,
01135: 
01136:         // ✅ guarda o que veio da API SOMENTE quando fizer sentido
01137:         // (PT_RIO HH:09 vira "", então cai em null)
01138:         close_hour_raw: closeRaw || null,
01139: 
01140:         prizesCount: prizes.length,
01141:         importedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
01142:       },
01143:       { merge: true }
01144:     );
01145:     ops++;
01146:     totalDrawsUpserted++;
01147:     if (isNewDraw) totalDrawsSaved++;
01148: 
01149:     if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
01150: 
01151:     for (const p of prizes) {
01152:       const n = normalizePrize(p.value);
01153:       const prizeId = `p${String(p.position).padStart(2, "0")}`;
01154:       const prizeRef = drawRef.collection("prizes").doc(prizeId);
01155: 
01156:       let isNewPrize = false;

--- LINHAS 1168 A 1672 ---
01168:         {
01169:           position: p.position,
01170:           ...n,
01171:         },
01172:         { merge: true }
01173:       );
01174:       ops++;
01175:       totalPrizesUpserted++;
01176:       if (isNewPrize) totalPrizesSaved++;
01177: 
01178:       if (filterClose) proof.targetWriteCount += 1;
01179: 
01180:       if (ops >= 420) {
01181:         await batch.commit();
01182:         batch = db.batch();
01183:         ops = 0;
01184:       }
01185:     }
01186:   }
01187: 
01188:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01189: 
01190:   // ✅ flags finais de complete (por SLOT via query anti-índice)
01191:   if (proof.filterClose) {
01192:     const slotDate = proof.inferredDate; // se API trouxe o slot, teremos date
01193:     proof.expectedTargets = Math.max(1, Number(proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws || 0));
01194: 
01195:     if (slotDate) {
01196:       const { docs, complete } = await checkSlotCompletion({
01197:         date: slotDate,
01198:         closeHour: proof.filterClose,
01199:         lotteryKey: lk,
01200:       });
01201: 
01202:       proof.slotDocsFound = docs;
01203:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = complete;
01204:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = complete > 0;
01205:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = complete >= proof.expectedTargets;
01206:     } else {
01207:       proof.slotDocsFound = 0;
01208:       proof.alreadyCompleteCount = 0;
01209:       proof.alreadyCompleteAny = false;
01210:       proof.alreadyCompleteAll = false;
01211:     }
01212: 
01213:     // savedCount “forte” para o scheduler:
01214:     if (!proof.apiHasPrizes) {
01215:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
01216:     } else if (skipIfAlreadyComplete && proof.alreadyCompleteAll) {
01217:       proof.targetSavedCount = 0;
01218:     } else {
01219:       proof.targetSavedCount = proof.targetWriteCount;
01220:     }
01221:   }
01222: 
01223:   console.log(
01224:     `[IMPORT] API draws=${totalDrawsFromApi} | match_close=${totalDrawsMatchedClose} | validos=${totalDrawsValid}` +
01225:       ` | draws_upserted=${totalDrawsUpserted} | draws_novos=${totalDrawsSaved}` +
01226:       ` | prizes_upserted=${totalPrizesUpserted} | prizes_novos=${totalPrizesSaved}` +
01227:       ` | skip_vazio=${skippedEmpty} | skip_invalido=${skippedInvalid} | skip_close=${skippedCloseHour}` +
01228:       ` | skip_complete=${skippedAlreadyComplete}` +
01229:       ` | CHECK_EXISTENCE=${CHECK_EXISTENCE ? "ON" : "OFF"}` +
01230:       ` | uf=${uf || "NULL"} lottery_key=${lk}` +
01231:       (proof.filterClose
01232:         ? ` | expected=${proof.expectedTargets} complete=${proof.alreadyCompleteCount} slotDocs=${proof.slotDocsFound} apiTargets=${proof.apiReturnedTargetDraws}`
01233:         : "")
01234:   );
01235: 
01236:   return {
01237:     totalDrawsFromApi,
01238:     totalDrawsMatchedClose,
01239:     totalDrawsValid,
01240: 
01241:     totalDrawsSaved,
01242:     totalDrawsUpserted,
01243:

01256: /**
01257:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
01258:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)
01259:  * - Seguro: só apaga legado se existe UUID no slot
01260:  */
01261: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
01262:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01263:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
01264:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
01265: 
01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }
01305: 
01306:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01307: 
01308:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
01309: }

00292: function pad4(s) {
00293:   return String(s ?? "").trim().padStart(4, "0");
00294: }
00295: 
00296: /** Regra do Jogo do Bicho: dezena 00 => grupo 25; senão ceil(dezena/4) */
00297: function dezenaToGrupo(dezena2) {
00298:   const n = Number(dezena2);
00299:   if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
00300:   if (n === 0) return 25;
00301:   return Math.ceil(n / 4);
00302: }

00304: function normalizePrize(raw) {
00305:   const milhar = pad4(raw);
00306:   const centena = milhar.slice(-3);
00307:   const dezena = milhar.slice(-2);
00308:   const grupo = dezenaToGrupo(dezena);
00309:   const animal = grupo ? GRUPO_TO_ANIMAL[grupo] : null;
00310:   return { raw: String(raw), milhar, centena, dezena, grupo, animal };
00311: }

00313: function isISODate(s) {
00314:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00315: }
00316: 
00317: function isHHMM(s) {
00318:   return /^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00319: }
00320: 
00321: /**
00322:  * Normaliza horário para HH:MM (raw)
00323:  * Aceita: "10", "10h", "10:9", "10:09"
00324:  */
00325: function normalizeHHMM(value) {
00326:   const s = String(value ?? "").trim();
00327:   if (!s) return "";
00328: 
00329:   if (isHHMM(s)) return s;
00330: 
00331:   const m1 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
00332:   if (m1) return `${String(m1[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00333: 
00334:   const m2 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
00335:   if (m2) return `${String(m2[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
00336: 
00337:   const m3 = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/);
00338:   if (m3) {
00339:     const hh = String(m3[1]).padStart(2, "0");
00340:     const mm = String(m3[2]).padStart(2, "0");
00341:     return `${hh}:${mm}`;
00342:   }
00343: 
00344:   return "";
00345: }
00346: 
00347: /**
00348:  * ✅ Normaliza close_hour para SLOT (campo de negócio)
00349:  * - Para PT_RIO: força minutos = 00 (09:09 -> 09:00)
00350:  * - Para outros: preserva raw
00351:  *
00352:  * Correção crítica:
00353:  * - A API do PT_RIO costuma devolver HH:09 como "marcação" e NÃO um minuto real.
00354:  * - Portanto, para PT_RIO, se raw terminar em ":09", tratamos como "sem minuto real"
00355:  *   e NÃO persistimos close_hour_raw (vira "").
00356:  *
00357:  * Retorna:
00358:  * - raw: HH:MM (normalizado) OU "" quando for "sintético" (PT_RIO HH:09)
00359:  * - slot: HH:MM (normalizado para o que o sistema deve usar em consultas/ID)
00360:  */
00361: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(value, lotteryKey) {
00362:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00363:   const raw0 = normalizeHHMM(value);
00364:   if (!raw0 || !isHHMM(raw0)) return { raw: "", slot: "" };
00365: 
00366:   // ✅ FEDERAL: slot de negócio é sempre 20:00, mas preserva o raw
00367:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
00368:     return { raw: raw0, slot: "20:00" };
00369:   }
00370: 
00371:   if (lk === "PT_RIO") {
00372:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
00373:     const mm = raw0.slice(3, 5);
00374: 
00375:     // ✅ se vier HH:09, é marcação (não minuto real) -> não grava close_hour_raw
00376:     const raw = mm === "09" ? "" : raw0;
00377:     return { raw, slot: `${hh}:00` };
00378:   }
00379: 
00380:   return { raw: raw0, slot: raw0 };
00381: }
00382: 
00383: /**
00384:  * ✅ Normaliza o parâmetro closeHour (do CLI/scheduler) e evita log enganoso.
00385:  * - Para FEDERAL: qualquer coisa vira slot=20:00, mas mantemos "requested" para log.
00386:  */
00387: function normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lotteryKey) {
00388:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00389:   const requested = closeHour ? normalizeHHMM(closeHour) : null;
00390:   const requestedIsValid = requested ? isHHMM(requested) : false;
00391: 
00392:   if (!requested) {
00393:     return { requested: null, slot: null, note: null };
00394:   }
00395:   if (!requestedIsValid) {
00396:     return { requested, slot: null, note: "invalid" };
00397:   }
00398: 
00399:   // FEDERAL: slot único 20:00 (não fingir que o usuário pediu 20:00)
00400:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
00401:     const slot = "20:00";
00402:     const note = requested !== slot ? `FEDERAL_slot_fixed(${requested}->${slot})` : null;
00403:     return { requested, slot, note };
00404:   }
00405: 
00406:   // outros: normalização normal por lotteryKey (PT_RIO min->00 etc.)
00407:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(requested, lk).slot;
00408:   return { requested, slot: slot || null, note: null };
00409: }

00411: /**
00412:  * Sanitiza string para docId do Firestore.
00413:  */
00414: function safeIdPart(input) {
00415:   const s = String(input ?? "").trim();
00416:   if (!s) return "NA";
00417:   return s
00418:     .replace(/\s+/g, "_")
00419:     .replace(/[\/\\?#\[\]]/g, "_")
00420:     .replace(/:+/g, "-")
00421:     .slice(0, 80);
00422: }

00713: /**
00714:  * ✅ Merge + dedup de draws vindos de múltiplos requests
00715:  * Dedup key: date + close_hour(SLOT) + lottery_id (se existir)
00716:  */
00717: function mergeAndDedupDraws(arrays, lotteryKey) {
00718:   const map = new Map();
00719: 
00720:   for (const a of arrays) {
00721:     const list = Array.isArray(a) ? a : [];
00722:     for (const d of list) {
00723:       const date = String(d?.date || "").trim();
00724:       const { slot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d?.close_hour || "", lotteryKey);
00725: 
00726:       const lid =
00727:         pickLotteryId(d) ||
00728:         String(d?.lottery_name || d?.name || "").trim() ||
00729:         "NA";
00730:       const key = `${date}__${slot}__${lid}`;
00731: 
00732:       if (!map.has(key)) {
00733:         map.set(key, d);
00734:       } else {
00735:         // se duplicado, preferir o que tiver mais prizes preenchidos
00736:         const prev = map.get(key);
00737:         const prevCount = countPrizesInDraw(prev);
00738:         const curCount = countPrizesInDraw(d);
00739:         if (curCount > prevCount) map.set(key, d);
00740:       }
00741:     }
00742:   }
00743: 
00744:   return Array.from(map.values());
00745: }
00746: 
00747: function countPrizesInDraw(draw) {
00748:   let c = 0;
00749:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
00750:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
00751:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
00752:     c++;
00753:   }
00754:   return c;
00755: }

00763:   return Array.from(set).sort();

00908: /**

00913: async function checkAlreadyComplete(drawRef) {
00914:   try {
00915:     const dSnap = await drawRef.get();
00916:     if (!dSnap.exists) return false;
00917: 
00918:     const data = dSnap.data() || {};
00919:     if (Number(data.prizesCount) > 0) return true;
00920: 
00921:     // fallback: olha 1 doc de prizes
00922:     const pSnap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").limit(1).get();
00923:     return !pSnap.empty;
00924:   } catch {
00925:     return false;
00926:   }
00927: }

00938: async function checkSlotCompletion({ date, closeHour, lotteryKey }) {
00939:   try {

00942:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };

00950:       const d = doc.data() || {};

00952:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00953:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00954: 
00955:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;

00958:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;

01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }
01305: 
01306:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01307: 
01308:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
01309: }

00021: (function loadEnvLocal() {
00022:   try {
00023:     const envPath = path.join(__dirname, "..", ".env.local");
00024:     if (!fs.existsSync(envPath)) return;
00025: 
00026:     let raw = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
00027:     raw = raw.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ""); // remove BOM invisível
00028: 
00029:     raw.split(/\r?\n/).forEach((line) => {
00030:       let s = String(line || "").trim();
00031:       if (!s || s.startsWith("#")) return;
00032: 
00033:       // suporta "export KEY=VAL"
00034:       if (/^export\s+/i.test(s)) s = s.replace(/^export\s+/i, "").trim();
00035: 
00036:       const i = s.indexOf("=");
00037:       if (i <= 0) return;
00038: 
00039:       const k = s.slice(0, i).trim();
00040:       let v = s.slice(i + 1).trim();
00041: 
00042:       // suporta KEY="valor" / KEY='valor'
00043:       if (
00044:         (v.startsWith('"') && v.endsWith('"')) ||
00045:         (v.startsWith("'") && v.endsWith("'"))
00046:       ) {
00047:         v = v.slice(1, -1);
00048:       }
00049: 
00050:       if (k && v && !process.env[k]) process.env[k] = v;
00051:     });
00052:   } catch {
00053:     // silencioso por design
00054:   }
00055: })();

00057: /**
00058:  * Resolve automaticamente credenciais se:
00059:  * - GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS estiver ausente, OU
00060:  * - estiver apontando para um arquivo inexistente.
00061:  *
00062:  * Mantém compatível com seu fluxo local (Windows) e com Cloud (ADC).
00063:  */
00064: (function ensureGoogleCredentials() {
00065:   try {
00066:     const current = String(process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS || "").trim();
00067: 
00068:     // se já está OK, não mexe
00069:     if (current) {
00070:       try {
00071:         if (fs.existsSync(current) && fs.lstatSync(current).isFile()) return;
00072:       } catch {
00073:         // segue para fallback
00074:       }
00075:     }
00076: 
00077:     // se existe mas está inválida, remove para permitir fallback (ADC)
00078:     if (current) {
00079:       try {
00080:         delete process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS;
00081:       } catch {
00082:         process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = "";
00083:       }
00084:     }
00085: 
00086:     const tryPickFromDir = (dir) => {
00087:       try {
00088:         if (!dir || !fs.existsSync(dir)) return null;
00089: 
00090:         const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir);
00091:         const hits = entries
00092:           .filter((f) => /^palpitacojb-app-firebase-adminsdk-.*\.json$/i.test(f))
00093:           .map((f) => path.join(dir, f))
00094:           .filter((p) => {
00095:             try {
00096:               return fs.existsSync(p) && fs.lstatSync(p).isFile();
00097:             } catch {
00098:               return false;
00099:             }
00100:           });
00101: 
00102:         if (!hits.length) return null;
00103: 
00104:         hits.sort((a, b) => {
00105:           try {
00106:             return fs.statSync(b).mtimeMs - fs.statSync(a).mtimeMs;
00107:           } catch {
00108:             return 0;
00109:           }
00110:         });
00111: 
00112:         return hits[0] || null;
00113:       } catch {
00114:         return null;
00115:       }
00116:     };
00117: 
00118:     // 1) tenta no CWD
00119:     const cwdPick = tryPickFromDir(process.cwd());
00120:     if (cwdPick) {
00121:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = cwdPick;
00122:       return;
00123:     }
00124: 
00125:     // 2) tenta subir alguns níveis
00126:     const up1 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", ".."); // .../archive_backend
00127:     const up2 = path.resolve(__dirname, "..", "..", "..", ".."); // .../palpitaco (provável)
00128:     const pick1 = tryPickFromDir(up2) || tryPickFromDir(up1);
00129: 
00130:     if (pick1) {
00131:       process.env.GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS = pick1;
00132:       return;
00133:     }
00134:   } catch {
00135:     // silencioso por design
00136:   }
00137: })();

00940:     const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00941:     const targetSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
00942:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00943: 
00944:     // query mínima
00945:     const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
00946:     if (snap.empty) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00947: 
00948:     const refs = [];
00949:     snap.forEach((doc) => {
00950:       const d = doc.data() || {};
00951: 
00952:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00953:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00954: 
00955:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;
00956: 
00957:       // ✅ compara por SLOT normalizado (pega legado "09:09" como "09:00")
00958:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;
00959:       if (storedSlot !== targetSlot) return;
00960:

00962:     });

00964:     if (!refs.length) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };

00967:     for (const ref of refs) {
00968:       const ok = await checkAlreadyComplete(ref);
00969:       if (ok) complete += 1;
00970:     }
00971: 
00972:     return { docs: refs.length, complete };
00973:   } catch {
00974:     return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
00975:   }
00976: }

01261: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
01262:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01263:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
01264:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
01265: 
01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }

01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }

00387: function normalizeRequestedCloseHour(closeHour, lotteryKey) {
00388:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00389:   const requested = closeHour ? normalizeHHMM(closeHour) : null;
00390:   const requestedIsValid = requested ? isHHMM(requested) : false;
00391: 
00392:   if (!requested) {
00393:     return { requested: null, slot: null, note: null };
00394:   }
00395:   if (!requestedIsValid) {
00396:     return { requested, slot: null, note: "invalid" };
00397:   }
00398: 
00399:   // FEDERAL: slot único 20:00 (não fingir que o usuário pediu 20:00)
00400:   if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
00401:     const slot = "20:00";
00402:     const note = requested !== slot ? `FEDERAL_slot_fixed(${requested}->${slot})` : null;
00403:     return { requested, slot, note };
00404:   }
00405: 
00406:   // outros: normalização normal por lotteryKey (PT_RIO min->00 etc.)
00407:   const slot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(requested, lk).slot;
00408:   return { requested, slot: slot || null, note: null };
00409: }

00421:     .slice(0, 80);

01256: /**
01257:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
01258:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)

01261: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
01262:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01263:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
01264:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
01265: 
01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }
01305: 
01306:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01307: 
01308:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
01309: }

00717: function mergeAndDedupDraws(arrays, lotteryKey) {
00718:   const map = new Map();
00719: 
00720:   for (const a of arrays) {
00721:     const list = Array.isArray(a) ? a : [];
00722:     for (const d of list) {
00723:       const date = String(d?.date || "").trim();
00724:       const { slot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d?.close_hour || "", lotteryKey);
00725: 
00726:       const lid =
00727:         pickLotteryId(d) ||
00728:         String(d?.lottery_name || d?.name || "").trim() ||
00729:         "NA";
00730:       const key = `${date}__${slot}__${lid}`;
00731: 
00732:       if (!map.has(key)) {
00733:         map.set(key, d);
00734:       } else {
00735:         // se duplicado, preferir o que tiver mais prizes preenchidos
00736:         const prev = map.get(key);
00737:         const prevCount = countPrizesInDraw(prev);
00738:         const curCount = countPrizesInDraw(d);
00739:         if (curCount > prevCount) map.set(key, d);
00740:       }
00741:     }
00742:   }
00743: 
00744:   return Array.from(map.values());
00745: }
00746: 
00747: function countPrizesInDraw(draw) {
00748:   let c = 0;
00749:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
00750:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
00751:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
00752:     c++;
00753:   }
00754:   return c;
00755: }

00757: function summarizeCloseHours(draws, lotteryKey) {
00758:   const set = new Set();
00759:   for (const d of draws) {
00760:     const { slot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d?.close_hour || "", lotteryKey);
00761:     if (slot) set.add(slot);
00762:   }
00763:   return Array.from(set).sort();
00764: }

01256: /**
01257:  * ✅ Cleanup automático: remove docs legados (lottery_id null) quando existe doc UUID no mesmo date+slot.
01258:  * - Usa query mínima por date (sem índice composto)

01261: async function cleanupLegacyDrawsForDate({ date, lotteryKey }) {
01262:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
01263:   if (!date || !isISODate(date) || !lk)
01264:     return { ok: false, deleted: 0, slots: 0, reason: "bad_args" };
01265: 
01266:   const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
01267:   const rows = snap.docs.map((doc) => ({ ref: doc.ref, id: doc.id, ...doc.data() }));
01268: 
01269:   const mine = rows.filter(
01270:     (r) => String(r.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase() === lk
01271:   );
01272: 
01273:   const bySlot = new Map();
01274:   for (const r of mine) {
01275:     const slot = String(r.close_hour || "").trim();
01276:     if (!slot) continue;
01277:     if (!bySlot.has(slot)) bySlot.set(slot, []);
01278:     bySlot.get(slot).push(r);
01279:   }
01280: 
01281:   let deleted = 0;
01282:   let slots = 0;
01283:   let batch = db.batch();
01284:   let ops = 0;
01285: 
01286:   for (const [slot, arr] of bySlot.entries()) {
01287:     const hasUuid = arr.some((x) => x.lottery_id && String(x.lottery_id).includes("-"));
01288:     if (!hasUuid) continue;
01289: 
01290:     const leg = arr.filter((x) => !x.lottery_id);
01291:     if (!leg.length) continue;
01292: 
01293:     for (const d of leg) {
01294:       batch.delete(d.ref);
01295:       deleted += 1;
01296:       ops += 1;
01297:       if (ops >= 450) {
01298:         await batch.commit();
01299:         batch = db.batch();
01300:         ops = 0;
01301:       }
01302:     }
01303:     slots += 1;
01304:   }
01305: 
01306:   if (ops > 0) await batch.commit();
01307: 
01308:   return { ok: true, deleted, slots };
01309: }

00747: function countPrizesInDraw(draw) {
00748:   let c = 0;
00749:   for (let i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
00750:     const v = draw?.[`prize_${i}`];
00751:     if (v === null || v === undefined || String(v).trim() === "") continue;
00752:     c++;
00753:   }
00754:   return c;
00755: }

00757: function summarizeCloseHours(draws, lotteryKey) {
00758:   const set = new Set();

00760:     const { slot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d?.close_hour || "", lotteryKey);

00762:   }
00763:   return Array.from(set).sort();
00764: }

00768:  * - Por default faz 1 request por lotteryId e mescla (evita “cap”/consolidação do endpoint)
00769:  * - Se KING_FETCH_PER_LOTTERY=0 -> faz 1 request único (modo antigo)

00771: async function fetchKingResults({ date, lotteryKey }) {
00772:   const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
00773:   const lotteries = LOTTERIES_BY_KEY[lk];
00774:   if (!lotteries?.length) {
00775:     if (lk === "FEDERAL") {
00776:       throw new Error(
00777:         `FEDERAL sem lotteryIds. Defina KING_LOTTERIES_FEDERAL="uuid1,uuid2,..." para habilitar fetch.`
00778:       );
00779:     }
00780:     throw new Error(`lotteryKey inválida: ${lk}`);
00781:   }
00782: 
00783:   if (!FETCH_PER_LOTTERY) {
00784:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk });
00785:     return await axiosGetJson(url);
00786:   }
00787: 
00788:   // ✅ 1 request por lottery (sequencial: mais estável e respeita rate-limit)
00789:   const parts = [];
00790:   const errors = [];
00791: 
00792:   // helper local: extrai e normaliza closes vindos nesse UUID
00793:   const closeFromDraw = (d) =>
00794:     normalizeHHMM(d?.close_hour ?? d?.closeHour ?? d?.horario ?? d?.close ?? "");
00795: 
00796:   const summarizeCloseHoursPart = (list) => {
00797:     const set = new Set();
00798:     for (const d of Array.isArray(list) ? list : []) {
00799:       const c = closeFromDraw(d);
00800:       if (c) set.add(c);
00801:     }
00802:     return Array.from(set).sort();
00803:   };
00804: 
00805:   for (const lotteryId of lotteries) {
00806:     const url = buildResultsUrl({ date, lotteryKey: lk, lotteryId });
00807: 
00808:     try {
00809:       const data = await axiosGetJson(url);
00810: 
00811:       // ✅ carimba o UUID do request em cada draw (a API nem sempre retorna lottery_id)
00812:       if (data && Array.isArray(data.data)) {
00813:         data.data = data.data.map((d) => ({
00814:           ...d,
00815:           lottery_id: d?.lottery_id || lotteryId,
00816:           lotteryId: d?.lotteryId || lotteryId,
00817:         }));
00818:       }
00819:       const list = Array.isArray(data?.data) ? data.data : [];
00820:       parts.push(list);
00821: 
00822:       if (KING_FETCH_DEBUG) {
00823:         const closesPart = summarizeCloseHoursPart(list);
00824:         console.log(
00825:           `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} draws=${list.length} close_hours=[${closesPart.join(
00826:             ", "
00827:           )}]`
00828:         );
00829:       }
00830:     } catch (e) {
00831:       const msg = e?.response?.status
00832:         ? `HTTP_${e.response.status}`
00833:         : e?.code || e?.message || "ERR";
00834: 
00835:       errors.push({ lotteryId, msg });
00836: 
00837:       console.warn(
00838:         `[FETCH:PART] ${lk} ${date} lotteryId=${lotteryId} ERROR=${msg} (seguindo...)`
00839:       );
00840: 
00841:       // segue para não travar o dia inteiro por 1 UUID
00842:       continue;
00843:     }
00844:   }
00845: 
00846:   const merged = mergeAndDedupDraws(parts, lk);
00847:   const closes = summarizeCloseHours(merged, lk);
00848:   const errInfo =
00849:     KING_FETCH_DEBUG && errors.length
00850:       ? ` errors=${errors.length} ids=[${errors.map((x) => x.lotteryId).join(",")}]`
00851:       : "";
00852: 
00853:   console.log(
00854:     `[FETCH] ${lk} ${date} per-lottery=${lotteries.length} -> merged_draws=${
00855:       merged.length
00856:     } close_hours=[${closes.join(", ")}]${errInfo}`
00857:   );
00858:   return { success: true, data: merged };
00859: }
